<meta>This page marks the beginning of the author's preface (Signature A 2). The text introduces a common 17th-century alchemical theme: that God is revealing long-hidden secrets of nature to the world as it enters its final ages.</meta>

<image-desc>An ornamental typographic headpiece consisting of several rows of fleurons. The central motif is a royal crown resting on a platform, flanked by symmetrical patterns of stylized birds and floral designs.</image-desc>

# JOHANNES DE MONTE-SNYDERS
<note>Johannes de Monte-Snyders (c. 1625–1670) was a famous wandering alchemist and author known for his works on the "Universal Medicine" and the transmutation of metals.</note>

## Preface,

### Gracious Reader:

Since it has now pleased God the Almighty in these latter times <note>The "latter times" refers to the contemporary religious belief that the world was in its final era, a period when many believed God would finally reveal the secret workings of nature to the faithful.</note> to let His grace rain upon the ungodly as well as the righteous, and since He is determined to spread His secret, hidden wisdom throughout the whole world, and to that end has sent forth His holy angel to use a trumpet blast to awaken the spirit of all true philosophers <note>In the context of this book, "philosophers" specifically refers to alchemists or "philosophers of fire" who sought the hidden truths of creation.</note>, which until now has likewise been in a—

<meta>catchword: nem</meta>

<summary>In this preface, the alchemist Johannes de Monte-Snyders announces that God is now revealing the hidden secrets of the universe to all mankind, signaling a new era of spiritual and scientific enlightenment.</summary>

<keywords>Johannes de Monte-Snyders, alchemy, divine revelation, hidden wisdom, latter times, natural philosophy</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the sentence from page 3, describing the awakening of the philosophical spirit from its long dormancy. The catchword "nem" from the previous page completes the word "einem" (a).</meta>

# PREFACE.

—deep sleep and remained hidden in the innermost reaches of this world, with the express command to bestow that same spirit upon others. Thus the time has now come—not that the deceased philosophers <note>The author refers to the great alchemical masters of antiquity and the Middle Ages.</note> should visibly rise again in their physical bodies, but rather that their spirit alone be awakened and, through grace, be bestowed upon others. This is so that the goodness of the Most High might be known throughout the whole world, and that wisdom might be made known to all those who wish to accept it, without distinction, and *regardless of prior merit* <note>original: "non prævisis meritis"; a theological term meaning that God’s grace is given freely, not because the recipient earned it through their own actions.</note>.

Since the lot has now fallen to me, the most unworthy of all, I am driven to fulfill the will of the Most High in true righteousness without delay. To that end, in the name of my God, I act as a sower, casting the **Universal Seed** <note>In alchemy, the "Universal Seed" refers to the fundamental substance or "prima materia" from which all metals and life forms are believed to grow.</note> which the philosophical spirit has communicated to me, into all the world, upon stony, sandy, thorny—

<meta>catchword: ground [te]</meta>

<keywords>Universal Seed, divine grace, philosophical spirit, spiritual awakening, Parable of the Sower, alchemy</keywords>

And so that I may now prove my calling before the whole world and make known that, by the command of **GOD**, the spirit of all philosophers speaks through me, and demonstrate that the keys of heaven and hell <note>A reference to the power of "binding and loosing," here applied to the alchemical secrets of dissolving and solidifying matter.</note> are entrusted to me through the Spirit of God: it is therefore highly necessary that I bring to light the *matter* <note>original: "materiam"; specifically the "Prima Materia" or the fundamental substance required for the Great Work of alchemy.</note> itself—that opening and closing key by which the mystery of this world and the wombs of all natures are opened, all things are impregnated, and brought forth to birth.

## METAMORPHOSIS
# OF THE PLANETS.
### Chapter I.
#### On the Three Worlds.

Just as all **elementary things** <note>original: *Elementata* — things composed of the four elements (earth, air, fire, water)</note> are divided into three kingdoms, just so are there three **irrational worlds** to be **considered philosophically** <note>original: *Mundi irrationales Philosophice consideriren*</note>; **that which is above is like that which is below** <note>original: *illud, quod est superius, est sicut illud, quod est inferius* — this is the most famous axiom of alchemy, from the Emerald Tablet of Hermes Trismegistus</note>. This comes about because the Almighty initially created the translucent **heavens** in a round, perfect, circular form, and these same heavens were likewise natured to bring forth their own kind, and this according to the command of **GOD** with more perfection; from this, there have now arisen and appeared in the heavens two lights, which [possess] the **shape** and **character** <note>original: *Figuram und Characterem*</note> of the circular crystalline

<summary>The author introduces the alchemical "Three Worlds" and invokes the Hermetic principle of "As above, so below." He describes the creation of the heavens in a perfect circular form, which led to the birth of the two great lights (the Sun and Moon).</summary>

<keywords>Three Worlds, Hermetic Principle, As above so below, creation, heavens, circular form, two lights, crystalline</keywords>

->METAMORPHOSIS<-

**heavens** <note>The text continues from the previous page: "crystalline heavens."</note> which place themselves before our eyes by day and night, which, as it were, proclaim and make known their Author <note>original: *Urheber* — referring to the Creator</note>. And just as the Almighty Creator <note>original: *Schöpffer*</note> reflected Himself in the **celestial world** <note>original: *Mundo cœlesti*</note> during the creation of the heavens, and that same world likewise brought forth the likeness of its Creator through two other lights, **so also God depicted Himself in the lower elemental world** <note>original: *sic quoque in Mundo Elementali inferiori se Deus depinxit*</note>; this coarse world was likewise originated in a circular form from the upper world through the **Word** <note>original: *verbum*</note> **"LET IT BE"** <note>original: *FIAT* — the divine command of creation from the Book of Genesis</note>. And so that this [world] might conform to the upper world in all things, the coarse elemental lower world has also depicted itself in two small worlds and brought forth its own kind; this **product** <note>original: *productum*</note> is **Masculine** and **Feminine** <note>original: *Masculinum* and *Fœmininum*</note>, just as in the **celestial world** <note>original: *Mundo cœlesti*</note> there are the two great lights which came forth by the command of the Almighty. The Sun is masculine and **diurnal** <note>original: *diurnalisch* — belonging to the day</note>, while the **Moon** <note>original: *Luna*</note> is feminine and **nocturnal** <note>original: *nocturnalisch* — belonging to the night</note>. **feminine seed**, especially the **metals**, which are formed by the **Masculine and Feminine Product** <note>original: *Productum Masculinum & Fœmininum*</note> in **their workshops** <note>original: *in officinis suis* — a term often used in alchemy to refer to the natural "laboratories" within the earth where minerals grow</note>, which I call in my **little book** <note>original: *libello*</note> the generated **irrational small worlds**, because these are marked with the **character** <note>original: *Character* — in this context, a "signature" or mystical mark that reveals an object's inner nature</note> and figure of their mother, the **Great World** <note>original: *grossen Welt* — the macrocosm</note>, whom she brought forth at God’s command. The more closely a birth agrees in form, figure, power, and property with its mother, the more magnificent it is to be esteemed; for parents, out of an **excess of singular love** <note>original: *per excessum singularis amoris*</note>, are accustomed to fashion their own children one by one, and to model and shape themselves within them, so much so that one often cannot distinguish the parents, especially the mother, from among her various children. Everything born in the **ordinary way** <note>original: *via ordinaria*</note> has come from **Male and Female** <note>original: *ex Mare & Fœmina*</note>, particularly among **living creatures** <note>original: *Animalibus*</note>. establish, and also promote them to their **predestination** <note>original: *Prädestination*</note>. For the Heavens are an image of God, and the two lights <note>The Sun and the Moon</note> are born and created **in the likeness of the image of Divine Majesty** <note>original: *ad similitudinem imaginis Divinæ Majestatis*</note>. And so that I may prove that the lowest is even as the highest <note>A reference to the "Emerald Tablet" of Hermes Trismegistus, a foundational text for alchemists which claims the physical world mirrors the spiritual world</note>, know that God the Almighty depicted Himself here in the **Elemental World** <note>original: *Mundo Elementali* — the realm of the four elements: earth, air, fire, and water</note> in the same way; first, by creating the world in a round form, etc., **as a sign of His perfection and eternity** <note>original: *tanquam signum suæ perfectionis & æternitatis*</note>, etc. This world, once again **by the power of the command: "increase and multiply"** <note>original: *ex vi mandati: crescite & multiplicamini*</note>, has brought forth two small worlds **which bear the symbol of the Creator; for no creature, whether considered in a greater or lesser degree, lacks the character of its Author, and it corresponds particularly in the same way with Him from whom [it came]** <note>original: *qui gestant symbolum Creatoris, nam nulla creatura secundum magis & minus considerando caret charactere Auctoris sui convenitque particulariter eodem modo cum illo à quo*</note>, etc.

Now, one is a **Lunar** <note>original: *Lunarische*</note>, feminine, and nightly regent, while the other is masculine, **Solar** <note>original: *Solarisch*</note>, and a ruler of the day. These two were created not **in the likeness of God** <note>original: *ad similitudinem Dei*</note>, but rather **after the image—namely, in the likeness of the World** <note>original: *ad imaginem nempe ad similitudinem Mundi*</note>. Thus also, **from the one Adam, two genders** <note>original: *ab uno Adamo duo genera*</note> have arisen, from whom all human beings have been born—

## How the world is to be recognized by the world; that is, how one should distinguish the hermaphroditic, small, irrational, mineral world from the lunar female birth, and further, how that which is below resembles that which is above. <note>This title references the famous Hermetic maxim from the Emerald Tablet: "That which is below is like that which is above."</note>

### CHAPTER III.

## Concerning the Dual and Universal
## Nature of the Hermaphroditic
## Small World.

This teaching of mine will initially seem quite strange to many; however, if they pay proper attention to my writing, it will not appear so very odd to them, despite the fact that, regarding the lunar birth <note>original: "ratione der lunarischen Geburt"</note>, this seems to contradict my first treatise **On Magic Elements** <note>original: "Tractatu de Elementis Magicis"</note>: For I reported at that time that the earthly **Saturn** <term>Saturnus: the alchemical name for lead, associated with heaviness, darkness, and the beginning of the "Great Work"</term> or **Lead** <note>original: "Plumbum"</note> encloses and gives birth to the seed of Silver and the **Mercury** <term>Quecksilber: "living silver," the fluid, volatile principle of matter essential for transformation</term>; therefore, one finds much premature silver in the very depths of the **lead mines** <note>original: "Bleybergen"</note>, etc. This is true and is not contradicted by me now; rather, it serves here as an explanation of the former: that common, grey Saturn, as a **bastard** <term>Bankart: a term for an illegitimate child, used here metaphorically to describe a metal that is impure or a "degenerated" version of a higher form</term> of the

# METAMORPHOSIS

**right Saturn** <term>Saturnus: the alchemical name for lead, often considered the "base" starting point of the alchemical work</term> as such a nature participates in the feminine, lunar **World-Child** <term>Welt-Kind: a symbolic term for a primordial substance or universal soul from which the metals are born</term>; and when the salt of the defective Saturn is prepared for it, the lead-mountains, being silver-bearing, could yield a greater advantage. Now one can well conclude from where it comes that those who place their hope in the **Mercury** <term>Mercurius: the principle of fluidity and transformation</term> of this Saturn are deceived in their **opinion**. The Mercury of lead is not in itself <note>original: "per se"</note> sufficiently <note>original: "sufficienter"</note> disposed to give birth to the **Moon or the Sun** <note>original: "Lunam vel Solem," representing Silver and Gold</note>, but rather to a greater or lesser degree <note>original: "sed secundum majus & minus"</note>, according to how much or how little of the true World-Child—the **Virgin Mercury, Sulfur, and Fixed Salt**—it has participated in within the **workshops** <note>original: "in officinis," referring to the natural laboratories or "offices" within the earth where minerals form</note>. Common Mercury, which springs from its own **ore** <note>original: "minera"</note>, is purer and much nobler than the coarse Saturnine Mercury; for with the former—by the help of **Venus** <term>Venus: the alchemical name for copper</term> and the true **Hermaphroditic Spirit**, a far better process can be established than many self-styled, haughty, proud **scribblers** <note>original: "Federhense," literally "quill-hens," a derogatory term for those who only know alchemy through books</note> and **process-peddlers** <note>original: "Proceß-Krämer"</note> let themselves dream. There are various processes and **particulars** <note>original: "Particularia," referring to specific chemical recipes or partial transmutations</note> in which, among other items, the true **Matter** <note>original: "Materia"</note> of our work is indeed included. However, because several other contrary items are used therein...

...the true **Saturn** <note>original: "Saturni"; the alchemical name for Lead</note> as such a nature participates in the female lunar world-child <note>referring to the silver-like essence within lead</note>, and when the salt of infirm **Saturn** is disposed toward it, the lead-bearing mountains, which contain silver, could yield a greater advantage. The Mercury of lead is not **sufficiently disposed by itself** <note>original: "per se nicht sufficienter disponirt"</note> to give birth to the **Moon** <note>Silver</note> or the **Sun** <note>Gold</note>, but **according to more or less** <note>original: "secundum majus & minus"</note>, depending on how much or little it has participated in the workshops of the true world-child, the **Mercury of the Virgin, Sulfur, and Fixed Salt** <note>original: "Mercurii Virginis Sulphuris & Salis fixi"</note>. 

Common **Mercury**, which springs from its own **ore** <note>original: "minera"</note>, is purer and much nobler than the coarse Saturnine Mercury; for with this, through the help of **Venus** <note>the alchemical name for Copper</note> and the true **Hermaphroditic spirit** <term>Hermaphroditischer Geist: a dual-natured substance or force that combines masculine and feminine principles to achieve transformation</term>, a far better **process** <note>original: "Proceß"</note> can be established than many supposed, haughty, proud scribblers <note>original: "Federhense"; a derogatory term for those who only have theoretical knowledge</note> and recipe-mongers <note>original: "Proceß-Krämer"</note> would let themselves dream. However, because some other adverse things are used therewith...

...with many alleged **prejudices** <note>original: "præjudicia" — in this legalistic context, these are formal objections or preconceived judgments used to stall a legal process.</note>, in order to retard the **proceedings** <note>original: "Actum"</note>, and fully annul them. ### OF THE PLANETS

**The Symbol:** Through the transformation of the Elements, let the purified trinity become the unity <note>original: *per Elementorum conversionem ternarius purificatus fiat monas*; this refers to the alchemical process of reducing the four elements into the three principles (Sulphur, Mercury, Salt) and finally into the One Thing or Philosopher's Stone</note>.

**Lull** <note>Ramon Llull, a 13th-century Majorcan polymath; though he likely didn't practice alchemy, many influential alchemical texts were attributed to him in the centuries following his death</note>, accompanied by his three giants, lions, and dragons, said this:
> Water finally returns to harmony with fire <note>original: *Cum igne tandem in gratiam redit aqua*; a reference to the "chemical wedding" or the union of opposites—moist/cold water and dry/hot fire</note>.

**Paracelsus** <note>The famous and controversial 16th-century Swiss physician who revolutionized medicine and alchemy</note> bore the symbols of the **Sun and Moon** <note>original: *Signum Solis & Lunæ*; representing Gold and Silver, or the masculine and feminine principles</note> on either side of him. He held his left hand upon the pommel of his sword <note>Legend says Paracelsus carried the "Azoth," the universal cure or secret mercury, hidden in the hollow hilt of his sword</note> and spoke with proud, haughty gestures:
> Separate and lead to maturity <note>original: *Separate & ad maturitatem perducite*; a summary of the alchemical method: first divide the matter into its components, then ripen them toward perfection</note>.

All of these were followed by a man who was hated by many, yet was a true Philosopher. He carried the world in his heart, for the **Universal Character** <note>A mystical symbol or "signature" believed to encompass the essence of all nature</note> shone from his eyes. He presented himself as a **Benedictine monk** <note>This identifies the figure as Basilius Valentinus, the legendary author of *The Twelve Keys*, who was said to be a 15th-century monk, though his writings first appeared in the early 1600s</note>. At one side he had a three-headed dragon; at the other, a strong eagle. Upon the very top of his head rested the **Spirit of Mercury**, and in his mouth he kept safe the **Soul of the Sun** <note>original: *animam Solis*; often referring to the "tincture" or the essential seed of gold</note>. He crushed the **Sulphur of Saturn** <note>Saturn represents Lead; "Sophists" were those Paracelsians deemed "false" alchemists who worked only with common minerals without understanding the spiritual/philosophical truth</note> of the beginning sophists under his two feet; but the **Sulphur of Mars** <note>Iron</note> and **Venus** <note>Copper</note>, mixed with **Dragon’s Blood** <note>A term for a deep red resin or a specific stage in the chemical breakdown of mercury and acids</note>, he held in both hands. The **Salt of the Sun** <note>The "Salt" or physical body of gold</note> was not forgotten, according to its proper proportion. And as I gazed upon this Philosopher with particular longing, I heard a voice calling:

METAMORPHOSIS

### Chapter 10.

# How Mercury, After He Had Triumphed, Transformed Himself into the Mercury of the Philosophers.

After these events occurred—namely, that Jupiter was first condemned and Mercury triumphed, and the sentence was published, and it was decided how the philosophical writings were henceforth to be understood—Mercury became so proud that he dared to proclaim himself as the **Mercury of the Philosophers** <term>Mercurio Philosophorum|The 'Mercury of the Philosophers' is not common quicksilver, but a purified, 'living' essence believed to be the essential starting material for the Philosopher's Stone</term>. 

He had his hair and beard shaven clean and anointed his head with vinegar, and the vinegar was an adornment of his head. He pulled his feet toward himself so that they could not be seen, for he mourned like a peacock when it looks at its own feet <note>In traditional animal lore, the peacock is proud of its feathers but ashamed of its ugly feet; this suggests Mercury is hiding his 'common' or 'earthly' origins now that he has attained a higher status</note>. Because of this, he froze and hardened like a **mineral of true Saturn** <term>minera veri Saturni|Saturn traditionally represents lead; the 'ore of true Saturn' often refers to antimony, a mineral used by alchemists to purify gold</term>. 

For he possesses a **solar sulfur** <note>original: *solarischen Sulphur*; sulfur associated with the Sun or Gold, representing the active, soul-like principle in alchemy</note> and a **martial spirit** <note>original: *Martialischen Geist*; 'martial' refers to Mars or Iron, signifying a sharp, penetrating, or catalytic strength</note> endowed with a dual nature. Indeed, the power of the Most High is within him, and the **Christian Wood** <note>original: *lignum Christianum*; likely a symbolic reference to the Cross or the 'Tree of Life,' suggesting that this alchemical process has a divine or universal spiritual significance</note> testifies to his universality.

At this, the Commander <note>likely representing God or the Master Alchemist directing the process</note> was angered, and he awakened a pleasant enemy <note>the "pleasant enemy" is a classic alchemical paradox, referring to a substance that "fights" another to refine it</note> for the dragon, as follows.

# CHAPTER XIII.

## How the true natural Venus, out of despair, threw herself into a deep water, and how she was rescued by the Eagle, and also transformed into the highest character, and what occurred further.

After these things had happened with Venus, as recounted above, the pale, desolate Venus went to a barren, desert place. There was a stagnant, deep water there, in which the sad Venus gazed at herself for a long time; and after she perceived a change in her form within the water, she was miraculously driven—by means of an inverted representation of the figure <note>original: "per inversam repræsentationem figuræ"; referring to her reflection in the water being upside down</note>—to join herself to her own shadow through precipitation <note>original: "per præcipitationem"; in alchemy, this refers to a solid settling out of a liquid solution</note>.

But the flying Eagle <note>The Eagle often symbolizes the "volatile" part of a substance, such as purified mercury or a spirit that rises during heating</note>, who previously had obtained his principal virtue from Venus, sensed well that the Dragon had distressed Venus; for this reason, he quickly reached out

# TRANSFORMATION <note>original: "METAMORPHOSIS"</note>

## CHAPTER XIV.

### How Mars intended to seize this transformed Venus with the help of his *Pegasus*, and how *Phoebus* prevented it.

Mars saw the noble, exceedingly beautiful, newborn Queen from his high cliff. He still held the taste of their old dalliance <note>original: "Buhlschafft"; referring to the mythological and alchemical union of Mars (iron) and Venus (copper)</note> in fresh memory, and he took pleasure in his own thoughts. Yet he was not at ease, for it seemed to him that the noble, powerful Queen was many thousand times lovelier and more agreeable than before, when he had committed adultery <note>Latin: *adulterium*</note> with her. Because of this, he began to prophesy and spoke: "Look, the lustful Venus is now a pure virgin, who carries the seed of her own impregnation within herself <note>In alchemy, this refers to the "hermetic androgyne" or "Rebis," a substance that contains both male and female principles and can therefore multiply itself without external aid</note>." He spoke these and similar things to himself, plucked up his courage, mounted **Pegasus** <note>Latin: *Pegasum*; in this context, Pegasus may represent the volatile or "flying" nature of a substance that allows it to move through the apparatus</note>, and intended to reach and kidnap the noble Queen before she became aware of it; but envious fortune was [against] him <note>The sentence ends abruptly here, following the catchword "zu" which likely leads to "zuwider" (against/contrary) on the next page.</note>

...was made from an unlit fire, from a fiery air, and from a **vegetable salt** <term>Sal vegetabile: A salt derived from plant matter, believed by alchemists to contain the "growing" or "living" power of nature, unlike static mineral salts.</term>. He mixed these quite well and set the entire cave on fire with them. However, these two lovers <note>The "two lovers" are Phoebus (Gold/Sun) and the Queen (representing Mercury or a purified metallic state), whose union is a central stage of the alchemical process.</note> did not notice this, and even when they did become aware of it, they paid it even less heed, but instead remained together, until finally **Vulcan** <note>Vulcan, the god of fire, represents the external heat of the furnace required to "kill" the substances so they can be reborn.</note> repeatedly brought his prepared powder in through the entrance from above and, with the most intense heat of all, reduced them both to dust and ashes. ### CHAPTER XVI.

## How Mars, upon the advice of a **Philosopher** <term>Philosophus</term> intended to awaken a new **Venus** with the **soul** <gloss>anima</gloss> of his **transformed Venus**, wherein the **soul of the Sun** <gloss>anima Solis</gloss> was united; and wherein he failed; also how he forcibly took from **Vulcan** the dead **body** <gloss>corpus</gloss> in which the **clarified** <note>meaning: purified or spiritualized</note> body was hidden, etc.

...canus <note>This completes the name Vulcanus from the previous page.</note>, and said: "Jupiter <note>In alchemical symbolism, the planet Jupiter represents the metal tin.</note> had descended to present the scepter and crown to the **Queen of Salvation** <note>original: *Königin des Heyls*; a personification of the refined alchemical substance or the 'Philosopher's Stone,' which was believed to 'heal' base metals into gold.</note>, and subsequently to petition for the office of Marshal. For from this **Matter** <note>original: *Materia*</note>, from which this Mistress is born, all metals have been born; therefore, the colors of all metals must appear in the glass <note>The alchemical flask or 'Philosopher's Egg' where the transformation was observed.</note>, and one planet must depose and drive out the others from their office and virtue, until the most constant among all—through the beauty of **Venus** <note>original: *Veneris*; the planet Venus represents the metal copper.</note> and the blood-rich bravery of **Mars** <note>original: *Martis*; the planet Mars represents the metal iron.</note>—possesses the kingdom in peace."

This pleased **Mars** beyond all measure; he even wanted to force his way into the royal hall with violence, in order to present all his power, authority, and reputation to the Queen. But the well-practiced laboratory assistant **Vulcan** <note>original: *Laborant Vulcanus*; the god of fire and the forge, representing the alchemist's control over the furnace.</note> would not permit it; he said: "Beware, dear brother, that you do not touch the room, otherwise we would both be robbed of our hope. For remember, when one breaks the blossoms from the trees, no fruit can come from them afterward."

# METAMORPHOSIS

After he <note>Referring to Vulcan, the skilled laboratory assistant mentioned on the previous page.</note> had now satisfied **Mars** <note>original: *Martem*; in alchemy, the planet Mars represents the metal iron.</note> with this report, he then opened another portal and warmed the royal hall according to his wisdom; and behold, a great transformation was before their eyes, and especially in the royal chamber. For the goddess **Diana** <term>Diana<gloss>In alchemical allegory, the goddess Diana—associated with the Moon—represents the metal silver.</gloss></term> had come across the **glassy sea** <note>original: *gläserne Meer*; this refers to the liquid state of the chemicals inside the glass vessel.</note> and had secretly ascended the throne. She was clothed in silver garments, and her maidens stood behind her; because of the brilliance of **Diana** <note>original: *Dianæ*</note>, they dared not look upon her face, but they reached out and held the royal throne with their hands. The throne, however, was made of pure gold, and the Queen who sat upon it—though she had golden feet—had a silver-white appearance, so that the **golden nature** <note>original: *Güldigkeit*; the internal essence or quality of gold.</note> hidden within her could not easily be recognized.

<summary>Vulcan transitions the alchemical process to the "whitening" stage, personified by the goddess Diana (silver) appearing on a golden throne. This represents a state where the material has become silver-white on the outside, though its internal essence is becoming gold.</summary>

<keywords>Metamorphosis, Mars, Diana, silver, gold, albedo, alchemy, Vulcan, glassy sea</keywords>

## CHAPTER XVIII.

### How **Vulcan** opened the final gate, and how **Mars** was slain by the **King**, and what other strange, adventurous colors appeared, etc.

**Mars** <note>original: *Mars*; representing the metal iron.</note> had observed all this quite well, but dared not ask **Vulcan** <note>original: *Vulcanum*; the personification of alchemical fire or the master of the laboratory.</note> anything further; for he saw him occupied with the opening of the last gates. When these had opened, the fiery flames or glowing heat assailed the **Royal Palace** <note>original: *Palatium Regis*; the chemical vessel where the transformation occurs.</note> in all places, and there appeared many thousands of fiery mirrors, and every mirror represented the beauty of the one who was to reign without end. **Mars** looked about him and could not see his Queen, who had taken on the form of **Diana** <term>Diana<gloss>In alchemy, Diana represents the Moon and the metal silver (the "white" stage of the work).</gloss></term>, but found in her place the most wise **King Solomon** <note>Solomon represents the perfection of the work: the "Red King" or the Philosopher's Gold.</note>, sitting in his glory. Therefore, he desired of **Vulcan** that he might assist him and allow him to go into the transparent chamber <note>original: *durchscheinende Zimmer*; likely the glass flask or retort.</note>, so that he might prevent that—

Now that the mighty hero **Mars** had lost his life for the love of his **Venus**, and was slain by the spirit of the dragon <term>Dragon's spirit<gloss>In alchemy, the "dragon" often refers to a corrosive acid or a volatile "mercurial" gas that dissolves or "slays" the solid metals during a reaction.</gloss></term>—with which the royal hall was filled to a fourth part—then King **Solomon** revealed himself in his glory <note>original: *in sua gloria*</note>, sat upon his triumphal chariot <note>original: *Curru triumphali*</note>, and ruled in

# PLANETARUM.

Peace over all the world; the red and animated sea <note>referring to the *Mare Rubrum* or "Red Sea," an alchemical symbol for the final red tincture or the blood of the philosophical lion that perfects all things</note> was subject to the King, giving its wealth willingly at the proper time, etc.

In short, the King became powerful in peace; for he, as the final Monarch, had conquered the beauty of **Venus** <note>original: *Veneris*; representing the metal copper and its transformed essence</note> and the glorious bravery of **Mars** <note>original: *Martis*; representing the metal iron and its strength</note> through the help of the old dragon <term>Dragon<gloss>In alchemy, the dragon represents the volatile, corrosive "mercurial" spirit. To "tame" the dragon means to "fix" this volatile substance so it no longer destroys, but instead stabilizes and heals.</gloss></term>. For the dragon was now tamed and no longer filled with envy and enmity as it had been before, but instead was gracious beyond all measure to the inhabitants of this world <note>original: *habitatoribus hujus mundi*</note>, proclaiming to them salvation <note>original: *Heyl*; here meaning both spiritual well-being and the physical "health" or purity of perfected metals</note>, wealth, and health, etc.

In alchemical terms, this refers to the "star-like" internal essences or spirits of metals that give them their color and properties.</note> must dissolve; therefore even *Phoebus* <note>The sun god, representing Gold.</note> was overwhelmed by the power of this water, so that he was forced to become water <note>To liquefy or dissolve into the solution.</note> himself, though without any harm to his nature.

The vindictive *Mars* <note>The god of war, representing Iron.</note> knew this right well; he had experienced the *effect* <note>original: "Effectum."</note> upon his *Venus* <note>The goddess of love, representing Copper.</note> long ago. For that reason, in order to deceive *Phoebus*, he had devised and composed this scheme, considering that *Phoebus* had previously caught his *transmuted* *Venus* <note>original: "transmutirte Venus."</note> in a rock and had brought her to his will, as mentioned above, and dallied with her according to his pleasure.

At this point, the kind reader and lover of the art <note>The "lover" or "amateur" refers to a student of alchemy.</note> should take special note of how wonderfully *Mars* avenges himself. After *Mars*—out of love for his supposed *Venus* (I say "supposed," for she was *metamorphosed* <note>original: "metamorphoſiret."</note>)—had been punished and brought into servitude through the trickery of old *Vulcan* <note>The smith-god, representing the heat of the furnace or fire.</note>, with whose wife he had previously dallied and to whom he is still bound in love, he now seeks revenge. He first revenges himself on *Phoebus* because, when he <note>Mars</note> wished to steal *Venus* away, *Phoebus* hindered the theft and the boo-

The raw material when it is in the stage immediately preceding its final transformation.</note> in this manner, for it did not please my Creator otherwise. In all places, however, a special metamorphosis of the stars <note>original: "Metamorphosis astrorum." In alchemy, the "stars" are the internal spirits or essences of the metals.</note> is found, showing how the lesser, mediocre, worthy, and most worthy planets <note>metals</note>—through special manual operations, good fortune and misfortune, and otherwise through divine providence—have been transformed into the primary matter closest to us <note>original: "materiam primam nobis proximam." The fundamental substance of the universe as it exists in a state ready for the chemist to work with.</note>. ### CHAPTER XXIII.

## How *Phoebus* was cast down backward from the chariot; how *Pluto* beset him with his hellish poison; and how he was transformed into a three-headed worm; also how his brothers abandoned him and delivered him over to *Vulcan*, etc.

After **Diana** <note>original: *Diana*; the goddess of the moon, representing the metal Silver.</note> had risen to such high dignity through the design of **Cupid** <note>original: *Cupidinis*; the god of love, here representing the attraction between chemical substances.</note> and the advancement of the martial **Venus** <note>original: *Veneris*; the goddess of love, associated here with the "martial" or iron-influenced stage of copper.</note>, I heard these words being proclaimed from the four corners of the world to all rational creatures; it seemed to me to be the voice of the goddess **Diana, the child-bearer of worldly salvation** <note>original Latin: *Dianæ puerperæ secularis salutis*; a title suggesting the Moon/Silver is the precursor to the final alchemical goal.</note>, who spoke thus: **"I should perish, had I not perished,"** <note>original Latin: *Perirem, nisi periissem*; a classic alchemical maxim meaning that a substance must be destroyed or "die" in its base form to be reborn in a perfected state.</note>

<summary>Mercury provides a "holy oil" to strengthen the celestial figures before the Sun (Phoebus) undergoes a dramatic transformation involving a descent into the underworld and a symbolic death. This transition represents the "nigredo" or blackening stage of alchemy, where matter is broken down to be purified.</summary>

Nothing evil happens through which the most high **Creator** <note>original: *Schöpffer*</note> of all things cannot work some good. For behold, I was a weak vessel, a tender, fickle virgin; I loved chastity <note>original: *Keuschheit*; in alchemy, this often represents the purity of a metal before it is "corrupted" or combined with others.</note> and punished all those who sought to violate my honor or that of my followers. To **Actaeon** <note>original: *Actæon*; a hunter in Greek myth who saw Diana bathing and was turned into a stag. Alchemically, this represents the transformation of the observer or the matter itself when exposed to the lunar "solvent."</note>, because he saw me naked in the bath, I gave from my lunar horns, and he was torn to pieces, unrecognized by his most faithful hounds. For that reason, I was betrayed in another way against my will: for while I loved chastity most highly and punished unchastity <term>Unzucht</term> in others, I fell through my own curiosity <note>original: *Fürwitz*; a term implying a meddlesome or over-eager desire for forbidden knowledge.</note>. I feared that it would happen to me with them just as it did to the innocent **Actaeon** with his dear dogs. But my innocence and my own predestination <term>Prädestination</term> elevated me to this place, where henceforth no misfortune shall touch me.

# METAMORPHOSIS

The worm they saw had a head at the front, at the end, and also in the middle. These heads were not like one another in external appearance: for the foremost was of **Sulfur**, the hindmost of **Vitriol**, and the middle one originated from **Mercury**. <note>The author is describing the *Tria Prima*—the three primary substances of alchemy: Sulfur (the soul/inflammability), Mercury (the spirit/volatility), and Salt (the body/stability). Here, "Vitriol" stands in for the Salt or the fixed base.</note> This middle head had eyes in front and in back; it watched the foremost and hindmost heads with special diligence. From its ears came two hands, which endeavored to bring the hindmost and foremost heads together. <note>Mercury is known as the "mediator" or "ligament" that seeks to join the active Sulfur and the passive Salt/Vitriol.</note>

But an angel came, called **Uriel**, <note>Uriel, whose name means "Fire of God," often appears in alchemical texts as the agent of "calcination" or the transformative fire that purifies the matter.</note> who poured a fiery bowl upon the middle head of the worm and burned both its hands, so that it had to leave the foremost and hindmost heads and fly into the air. For wings grew from the place where the hands had been—hands that had not wanted to leave the **Sulfur** and the **Salt**. These wings carried the middle part into its own **mercurial kingdom**. From there, it will return across the **Glassy Sea** <note>original: *Mare Vitreum*. In the Bible (Revelation 4:6), this is the sea before the throne of God. In alchemy, it refers to the stage where the matter becomes clear, transparent, and spiritually "fixed."</note> to take up its office. And then the Most High will send forth His Spirit, and the two abandoned **solar parts**, <note>The "solar parts" are the remnants of the Sun/Gold that was broken down on the previous page; they must be purified as separate principles before they can be reunited into a higher form.</note> namely Sulfur and Salt, which [were] in a worm...

# METAMORPHOSIS
<note>Transformation</note>

Bloodthirsty **Mars** <note>representing the metal iron, known for its hardness and resistance to heat</note> feels the grace of his brother; and instead of Mars previously always resisting the fire and fighting with his fiery sparks against **Vulcan** <note>the personification of the alchemical fire and the blacksmith of the gods</note> who hammered him, he now places himself as humbly and mildly under the hammer as molten, common **Saturn** <note>representing the metal lead, which melts easily and is "mild" or soft compared to iron</note>. He is clothed inside and out, through and through, with golden pieces. **Jupiter** <note>representing the metal tin</note>, **Saturn**, and **Mercury** <note>representing quicksilver</note> all enjoy the golden food which their brothers have served to them; they forget thereby all the endured misery of their former lives and have become immortal from the aforementioned food—so long as they guard themselves against the untimely grapes and the true **Magical Elements** <note>original: *Magischen Elementen*</note>, etc., about which more is to be read in its proper place <note>original: *suo loco*</note> in the *Treatise on the Magical Elements*.

### CHAPTER XXVI.

## How Saturn, when he wished to test whether his brothers were improved in truth and transformed into the Sun, was anointed by command of the Master, and how, because of the superabundant Medicine, he transformed his brother Jupiter into the Sun, etc.
<note>In alchemy, "Sol" or the Sun represents gold. The "Medicine" refers to the Philosopher's Stone or Tincture, which has the power to "improve" or transmute base metals.</note>

He begged **Vulcan** <note>the personification of alchemical fire and the furnace</note> that he might, in order to learn the truth, be allowed to examine <note>original: *ad examinandum*</note> one of his brothers who claimed to have experienced the transformation <note>original: *Transmutation*</note> in his own body. The Almighty allowed this virtue to be granted to him through Vulcan <note>original: *per Vulcanum*</note>—at the moment he was meant to examine his brothers in the river—whereby he was transformed into a medicine. For this reason, Jupiter thanked his brother Saturn, who had shown him such grace unintentionally and by chance <note>original: *præter intentionem & fortuito*</note>. ...of the beast, upon which the beast fed for so long, until it became a truly poisonous white **Hydra** <note>A multi-headed serpent; in alchemy, this often represents a substance that is difficult to fix or a "mercurial" spirit that must be tamed</note>: And when **Vulcan** <note>The Roman god of fire, representing the heat of the alchemical furnace</note> fed the **Hydra** with the sacrifice of **Neptune** <note>The god of the sea, representing water or specialized salts</note> and **Venus** <note>The goddess of love, representing copper or the principle of attraction/union</note> anew and for the third time, then the **worm** <note>A term for the base, earth-bound form of the material</note> transformed itself, and it became a **bird**, which could cover the entire world with its wings; it had its claws spread wide like an **eagle**, bit into the ground with its beak, and seized the world from its rising to its setting <note>from East to West</note>, and the world, together with this flying poisonous beast, hid from my eyes, so that I could see nothing more than a heavy, thick **vapor**. This vapor moved by the grace of **Vulcan** <note>original Latin: *beneficio Vulcani*; referring to the continued application of heat</note> to climb toward the heights, but the heavens prevented this, so that they were forced to deviate to the side into the adjacent cavern <note>Likely referring to the side-arm or the glass walls of a still (alembic)</note>, which had no exit, but the entrance served them as an exit, etc.

## CHAPTER XXIX.

### How the philosophical virgin is born from the aforementioned clouds, and how she gives her milk and blood for the comfort of the needy, and also how to bring the soul into the metallic body, namely **Salt**, etc.

Now when these clouds had passed, I saw a beautiful white **virgin** <note>In alchemy, the "white virgin" often represents the purified, white stage of the matter, known as the *albedo*, which follows the "black" stage of decay</note> ascending from the earth; she pressed her breasts and made from her **virgin’s milk** <note>original German: *Jungfrauen-Milch*; a term for a white liquid precipitate or a specialized solvent used to "nourish" the growing alchemical substance</note> a healing **butter**, with which she wished to make the dead living, for she cried out with a clear voice: "Die, you who live; arise, you who are dead" <note>original Latin: *Moriemini vivi, surgite mortui*; an alchemical paradox referring to the "death" or dissolution of the base metal so that it may be "resurrected" as the Philosopher's Stone</note>, etc. 

After I looked quite closely at the aforementioned virgin, I saw that after her hard pressing—because she would not cease—a thick red **blood** <note>The transition from white milk to red blood signifies the shift from the White Work (silver) to the Red Work (gold), the final and most potent stage of the process</note> flowed from her breasts, for there was no more milk present. This blood contaminated the butter; therefore **Vulcan** <note>The Roman god of fire, representing the heat of the furnace</note> undertook to take the butter away, but the virgin cried out and said: The milk, the butter, and

->The **grey Dragon** <note>original German: *graue Drach*; in alchemy, the dragon often represents the volatile or "unfixed" matter, or a powerful solvent</note> is my father, and the **white Bird** <note>original German: *weisse Vogel*; likely referring to the *albedo* or white stage of the process, or the "spirit" that rises during sublimation</note> is my grandmother, and these are both children of brothers and sisters; the blood I have from my father, and the milk has sprouted from my mother, the **Hermaphroditic World** <note>original German: *Hermaphroditischen Welt*; the *Rebis*, or the "Two-Thing," representing the perfect union of male/solar and female/lunar principles</note>: Therefore, this milk must also become blood through the remaining power of the **Sulphurous Souls** <note>original German: *Sulphurischen Seelen*; Sulfur is the alchemical principle of fire, soul, and "active" force that binds the substance</note>.<-

When I had heard all this, I asked my spirits for advice. They told me that a royal treasure was hidden in this cellar; furthermore, that the wolf had devoured many rich passengers (who had entered the cellar to rest at night during times of war) along with their money. # METAMORPHOSIS

Let the same reader be hereby assured, as truly as I live through **GOD**, that he shall also quite truly experience and learn its *preparation*, the *universal solvent* <note>original Latin: *Menstrum Universale*; a substance believed to be capable of dissolving all other matter</note>, the true irreducible *reduction* into *prime matter through prime matter* <note>original Latin: *materiam primam per materiam primam*; the fundamental, formless base of all substance</note>, the *union* <note>original Latin: *Conjunction*; the marriage of chemical opposites</note> and *solidification* <note>original Latin: *Fixation*; the process of making a volatile substance stable under heat</note> into a most precious *medicine*, along with the *multiplication* <note>original Latin: *Augmentation*; increasing the potency of the alchemical stone</note> and *ripening* <note>original Latin: *Fermentation*; the final stage of the work where the substance is "yeasted" into gold or medicine</note> in this *little compendium* <note>original Latin: *compendiolo*; a brief summary or handbook</note>.

# Notice.

## Kind Reader!

It is not without weighty reasons that I have, here in this **treatise** <note>original Latin: *Tractatu*</note>, dealt with the **philosophical concentrated Being** <note>original: *philosophischen concentrirten Ente*; likely referring to the *Ens*, or the fundamental spiritual essence of matter</note>, etc., **by way of Metamorphosis** <note>original Latin: *per modum Metamorphosis*; a method of describing the chemical process as a series of transformations or "changes of shape"</note>: for I am minded—considering that all **philosophers** <note>meaning the alchemists of antiquity and the Middle Ages</note> have written **obscurely** and their **style** is unknown to the world—to open the **eyes of reason** for all lovers of truth through this, my **mode of writing**, so that the—

<summary>The author explains that he has intentionally written this work on the "philosophical essence" in a transformative style to provide clarity for seekers of truth, contrasting his approach with the intentionally obscure writings of earlier alchemists.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the publisher’s catalog of scientific and esoteric works, following the previous listing of works by Jacquin and Hollandus. It focuses on the prolific output of Johann Gottfried Jugel and other 18th-century texts on mining and alchemy.</meta>

**Jugel’s (Joh. Gottfr.)** newly expanded and improved *Subterranean Geometry* <note>original: "Geometria subterranea"</note>, or the underground art of measurement for mining and pit structures, otherwise commonly called the **art of mine surveying** <term>Markscheidekunst: the specialized branch of surveying used to determine the position and direction of underground tunnels and property boundaries in mines.</term>, in 3 separate main parts with 8 copper plates. Large quarto. 1773. 3 Reichsthalers, 8 Good Groschen.

—— **His Speaking Oracle** in its natural language, which discovers the secret of active nature in the mineral kingdom, presented to the whole world for examination in 13 experiments and 31 principles. Large octavo. 1773. 1 Reichsthaler, 8 Good Groschen.

—— **The Art of Mining, Smelting, and Fixing** <note>original: "Berg- Schmelz- und Figier- Kunst." "Fixing" refers to the alchemical process of making a volatile substance stable or solid.</note>. 3 parts. Large octavo. 1766. 1 Reichsthaler, 15 Good Groschen.

**Count Franciscus Onuphrius de Marsciano's** *The Instructed Beginner in Chemistry*. Octavo. 1751. 8 Good Groschen.

**Lullius Revived and Unveiled** <note>original Latin: "Lullius redivivus denudatus"</note>, or Lullius newly revived and thoroughly explained. Octavo. 1772. 10 Good Groschen. <note>This refers to Pseudo-Ramon Llull, a name under which many popular alchemical texts were published centuries after the actual philosopher's death.</note>

**The Hermetic North Star**, or faithful instruction and guidance on how to achieve mastery, by J. J. F. Octavo. 1771. 12 Good Groschen.

--- Page 199 ---
<meta>This page serves as the title page for a specific work following the previous publisher's catalog. It introduces a famous alchemical text originally written in the mid-16th century, here presented in a late 18th-century edition (1773) for a German-speaking audience.</meta>

-> Concerning <-
-> the natural <-

# Philosophy

-> and the <-

## Transformation of Metals

-> into <-

# Gold and Silver,

-> through the highest natural <-

## Secret and Masterpiece,

-> which is called the <-
-> Philosopher’s Stone <note>original Latin: "Lapidem Philosophorum"</note>, <-

## Three Treatises,

-> first written in the French language <-
-> by the highly learned gentleman <-

### DIONYSIUS ZACHARIUS,

-> a French nobleman and Doctor of Law, <-
-> who in the year 1550 <-
-> made the Stone <note>original Latin: "Lapidem"</note> himself, as he reports; <-
-> but now brought into the German language for the warning <-
-> and guidance of all art-loving <note>The "art" here refers to alchemy, often called the "Hermetic Art."</note> Germans onto the one true path <-
-> for transforming metals, and explained with brief <-
-> summaries, <-
-> by <-

### Master <note>The "M." stands for "Magister," a title for someone who has attained a Master of Arts degree.</note> Georg Forberger,

-> of Mittweida in Meissen. <-

---

-> Frankfurt and Leipzig, <-
-> published by Johann Paul Krauß. 1773. <-

<summary>This is the title page for a 1773 edition of three alchemical treatises by Dionysius Zacharius, a 16th-century French nobleman who claimed to have successfully created the Philosopher's Stone. The work was translated and annotated by Georg Forberger to guide German practitioners toward the "true path" of metallic transmutation.</summary>
<keywords>Dionysius Zacharius, Georg Forberger, Philosopher's Stone, alchemy, metallic transmutation, gold, silver, 18th-century publishing</keywords>
